function applyProgramWritePatch08172(target,patch){
  if(!target||!patch)return target;
  for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(patch)){if(value!==undefined)target[key]=value;}
  if(patch.commonReportFormUrl!==undefined)target.reportFormUrl=patch.commonReportFormUrl;
  if(patch.activityType!==undefined)target.type=patch.activityType;
  if(patch.nightLinked!==undefined)target.affectsAttendance=Boolean(patch.nightLinked);
  return target;
}

async function migrateAdminProgramOverridesToSheetOnce(){
  if(currentRoleId()!=="admin" || state.settings?.programSheetMigrationV07662 || !window.schoolBoard?.saveProgramOperation) return;
  const overrides=state.programOverrides&&typeof state.programOverrides==="object"?state.programOverrides:{};
  const programs=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.programs)?readonlyCache.programs:[];
  let changed=false, failed=false;
  for(const [id,override] of Object.entries(overrides)){
    if(!override || !["date","time","place","teacher"].some(key=>String(override[key]??"").trim())) continue;
    const program=programs.find(item=>String(item.id||"")===String(id));
    if(!program?.source) continue;
    try{
      const result=await window.schoolBoard.saveProgramOperation({role:"admin",source:program.source,programId:program.programId||program.courseId||"",sessionId:program.sessionId||"",date:override.date??program.date??"",time:override.time??program.time??"",place:override.place??program.place??"",teacher:override.teacher??program.teacher??""});
      if(!result?.ok) throw new Error(result?.reason||"시트 저장 실패");
      if(result?.programPatch)applyProgramWritePatch08172(program,result.programPatch);
      const kept={...override}; ["date","time","place","teacher"].forEach(key=>delete kept[key]);
      if(Object.keys(kept).length) state.programOverrides[id]=kept; else delete state.programOverrides[id];
      changed=true;
    }catch(error){ failed=true; console.warn("[UEP] 프로그램 로컬값 시트 이관 실패",id,error); }
  }
  if(!failed){ state.settings.programSheetMigrationV07662=true; changed=true; }
  if(changed) await save();
}

let readonlyRefreshInFlight=null;
async function refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force=true, rerender=true}={}) {
  if(readonlyRefreshInFlight)return readonlyRefreshInFlight;
  readonlyRefreshInFlight=(async()=>{
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync || !window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache)return false;
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok){
    const status=await ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify:true}).catch(()=>null);
    googleConnectionStatus={ok:Boolean(status?.authenticated),encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api',endpointReady:status?.endpointReady!==false};
  }
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok)return false;
  try {
    if(force){
      const sync=await window.schoolBoard.previewReadonlySync();
      if(!sync?.ok) throw new Error(sync?.reason||'UEP 학교 데이터 자동 갱신 실패');
    }
    const cached=await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
    if(!cached?.ok) throw new Error(cached?.reason||'UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.');
    readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data);
    googleConnectionError='';
    if(typeof studentBadgeCache!=='undefined'&&studentBadgeCache?.clear) studentBadgeCache.clear();
    updateDormOutingCount();
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    if(rerender && !setupWizardActive){
      const scrollY=window.scrollY;
      render(state.activePage||'dashboard');
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.scrollTo(0,scrollY));
    }
    return true;
  } catch(error) {
    googleConnectionError=error?.message||'UEP 학교 데이터 자동 갱신 실패';
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    return false;
  }
  })();
  try{return await readonlyRefreshInFlight;}finally{readonlyRefreshInFlight=null;}
}

function operationalPageNeedsRender08172(page){return OPERATIONAL_RENDER_PAGES08172.has(String(page||''));}
let operationalRefreshInFlight=null;
async function refreshOperationalCacheSilently({rerender=true}={}){
  if(operationalRefreshInFlight)return operationalRefreshInFlight;
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.previewOperationalSync)return refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender});
  operationalRefreshInFlight=(async()=>{
    if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok)return false;
    try{
      const result=await window.schoolBoard.previewOperationalSync();
      if(result?.requiresFullSync)return refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender});
      if(!result?.ok)throw new Error(result?.reason||'UEP 운영자료 갱신 실패');
      if(result.patch&&typeof result.patch==='object')readonlyCache={...(readonlyCache||{}),...result.patch,operationalSyncedAt:result.operationalSyncedAt||result.syncedAt||new Date().toISOString()};
      googleConnectionError='';updateTopSyncStatus();updateDormOutingCount();
      if(rerender&&state.activePage&&operationalPageNeedsRender08172(state.activePage))render(state.activePage);
      return true;
    }catch(error){googleConnectionError=error?.message||String(error);updateTopSyncStatus();return false;}
  })();
  try{return await operationalRefreshInFlight;}finally{operationalRefreshInFlight=null;}
}
function startReadonlyAutoRefresh(){
  if(readonlyAutoRefreshTimer) clearInterval(readonlyAutoRefreshTimer);
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok) return;
  readonlyAutoRefreshTimer=setInterval(()=>{
    if(document.hidden) return;
    readonlyAutoRefreshCycle+=1;
    const fullSyncedAt=readonlyCache?.syncedAt?new Date(readonlyCache.syncedAt).getTime():0;
    const fullAge=fullSyncedAt?Date.now()-fullSyncedAt:Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    const needsFull=!fullSyncedAt||fullAge>=READONLY_FULL_REFRESH_MAX_AGE_MS||readonlyAutoRefreshCycle%6===0;
    if(needsFull){
      const jitter=Math.floor(Math.random()*45000);
      setTimeout(()=>{if(!document.hidden)refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender:true});},jitter);
      return;
    }
    refreshOperationalCacheSilently({rerender:true});
  },READONLY_AUTO_REFRESH_MS);
}

function updateTopSyncStatus() {
  const button = $("#syncButton");
  if (!button) return;
  button.classList.remove("good", "warn", "bad");
  const label = button.querySelector("span");
  if (googleConnectionError) {
    button.classList.add("bad");
    if (label) label.textContent = "구글시트 오류";
    button.title = googleConnectionError;
    return;
  }
  if (readonlyCache?.students?.length && neisData?.ok && !neisData.offline) {
    button.classList.add("good");
    if (label) label.textContent = "동기화 정상";
    button.title = "UEP 구글시트와 NEIS 자료가 정상 연결되었습니다.";
    return;
  }
  if (readonlyCache?.students?.length) {
    button.classList.add("warn");
    if (label) label.textContent = neisData?.offline ? "NEIS 저장자료" : "NEIS 연결 대기";
    button.title = neisError || "UEP 구글시트는 연결되었고 NEIS 자료를 확인 중입니다.";
    return;
  }
  if (googleConnectionStatus?.ok) {
    button.classList.add("bad");
    if (label) label.textContent = "시트 권한 확인";
    button.title = googleConnectionError || "서비스 계정 이메일의 구글시트 공유 권한을 확인하세요.";
    return;
  }
  button.classList.add("warn");
  if (label) label.textContent = "인증 필요";
  button.title = "교사 이름·이메일 로그인과 기기승인 상태를 확인하세요.";
}

}

async function selectGoogleCredential() {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.selectGoogleCredential)
    return openOAuthGuide();
  const result = await window.schoolBoard.selectGoogleCredential();
  if (result?.canceled) return;
  if (!result?.ok) { googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "Google 서비스 계정 인증을 연결하지 못했습니다."; updateTopSyncStatus(); return toast(googleConnectionError); }
  googleConnectionError = "";
  googleConnectionStatus = await window.schoolBoard.googleCredentialStatus();
  const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
  if (cached?.ok) { readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data); googleConnectionError = ""; }
  else googleConnectionError = cached?.reason || "UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.";
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  startReadonlyAutoRefresh();
  toast(`${result.account || "Google 서비스 계정"}으로 UEP 구글시트에 연결했습니다.`);
  render("settings");
}

async function previewReadonlySync() {
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync)return toast("학교 공용 데이터 연결 기능을 찾지 못했습니다.");
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok){
    const status=await ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify:true}).catch(()=>null);
    googleConnectionStatus={ok:Boolean(status?.authenticated),encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api',endpointReady:status?.endpointReady!==false};
  }
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok)return toast("학교 공용 읽기 연결에 실패했습니다. 로그아웃 후 이름·이메일로 다시 로그인해 주세요.");
  const button = $("[data-readonly-sync]");
  if (button) {
    button.disabled = true;
    button.textContent = "읽는 중…";
  }
  let result=null;
  try{result=await window.schoolBoard.previewReadonlySync();uepDiagRecord('sync.preview.result',result);}
  catch(error){
    uepDiagRecord('sync.preview.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});
    result={ok:false,reason:error?.message||'학교 공용 자료 조회 예외'};
  }
  if (!result?.ok) {
    if (button) {
      button.disabled = false;
      button.textContent = "지금 새로고침";
    }
    googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "동기화하지 못했습니다.";
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    return toast(googleConnectionError);
  }
  googleConnectionError = "";
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  const p = result.preview;
  const n = result.nightPreview || {};
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "GOOGLE SHEETS";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent = "UEP 구글시트 조회 결과";
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="sync-preview"><div><b>${p.masterCount}</b><span>현재원</span></div><div><b>${p.nfcCount ?? "-"}</b><span>NFC 등록</span></div><div><b>${p.dormRosterCount ?? "-"}</b><span>현재 학사생</span></div><div class="${p.unmatchedCount ? "warn" : ""}"><b>${p.unmatchedCount || 0}</b><span>미결합 기록</span></div></div><h4 class="sync-section-title">업무자료 읽기 결과</h4><div class="sync-preview night-sync-preview"><div><b>${p.dormOutingCount ?? "-"}</b><span>학사외출</span></div><div><b>${n.attended ?? "-"}</b><span>야자 출석행</span></div><div class="${n.outsideQr ? "warn" : ""}"><b>${n.outsideQr ?? "-"}</b><span>명단 외 QR</span></div><div><b>${formatSyncTime(result.syncedAt)}</b><span>실시간 조회시각</span></div></div><div class="readonly-rule"><b>기준 구글시트</b><span>${escapeHtml(result.sourceName || readonlyCache?.sourceName || "2026_운호고_UEP_DB_v1.0_Dev")}</span></div><p class="drawer-note">02_학생마스터 학생ID를 기준으로 구글시트 각 탭을 실시간 결합했으며 원본은 변경하지 않았습니다.</p>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  toast("UEP 구글시트를 다시 읽었습니다.");
  if (window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache) {
    const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
    if (cached?.ok) readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data);
  }
  closeDrawer();
  render("dashboard");
}

async function retryGoogleConnection() {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync) return navigate("settings");
  toast("UEP 구글시트를 다시 읽는 중입니다.");
  const result = await window.schoolBoard.previewReadonlySync();
  if (!result?.ok) {
    googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "UEP 구글시트 연결에 실패했습니다.";
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    toast(googleConnectionError);
    render("dashboard");
    return;
  }
  const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
  if (cached?.ok) {
    readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data);
    googleConnectionError = "";
    toast("UEP 구글시트를 다시 불러왔습니다.");
  } else {
    googleConnectionError = cached?.reason || "UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.";
    toast(googleConnectionError);
  }
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  render("dashboard");
}

function updateConnectionControls() {
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  const connected = Boolean(readonlyCache?.students?.length && !googleConnectionError);
  const status = $(".connection-title .state");
  if (status && connected) {
    status.textContent = "구글시트 연결됨";
    status.classList.remove("warn", "error");
  }
  const sync = $("[data-readonly-sync]");
  if (sync) sync.disabled = !googleConnectionStatus?.ok;
}

function updateDashboardTimetableControls() {
  const card = $(".timetable-card");
  if (!card) return;
  card.querySelectorAll(".timetable-popup-actions").forEach(node=>node.remove());
}

function shiftTimetableDate(value, amountDays) {
  const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return dateKey(new Date());
  base.setDate(base.getDate() + Number(amountDays || 0));
  return dateKey(base);
}
function timetableWeekLabel(value) {
  const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return "주간 선택";
  const day = base.getDay();
  base.setDate(base.getDate() + (day === 0 ? -6 : 1 - day));
  const end = new Date(base); end.setDate(end.getDate() + 4);
  const md = d => `${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()}`;
  return `${md(base)} ~ ${md(end)}`;
}

  if (googleConnectionStatus?.ok) {
    button.classList.add("bad");
    if (label) label.textContent = "시트 권한 확인";
    button.title = googleConnectionError || "서비스 계정 이메일의 구글시트 공유 권한을 확인하세요.";
    return;
  }
  button.classList.add("warn");
  if (label) label.textContent = "인증 필요";
  button.title = "교사 이름·이메일 로그인과 기기승인 상태를 확인하세요.";
}

async function loadNeisDashboard(force = false, requestedDate = "") {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.loadNeisDashboard) {
    neisError = "이 실행환경에서는 NEIS 연결 기능을 사용할 수 없습니다.";
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    return;
  }
  if (neisLoading) return;
  neisLoading = true;
  if (state.activePage === "dashboard" || state.activePage === "timetable" || state.activePage === "settings") render(state.activePage);
  const options = {
    schoolName: state.settings.neis.schoolName,
    officeCode: state.settings.neis.officeCode,
    schoolCode: state.settings.neis.schoolCode,
    grade: state.settings.neis.grade,
    classNo: state.settings.neis.classNo,
    date: requestedDate || dateKey(new Date()),
    useApiKey: Boolean(state.settings.neis.useApiKey),
    comciganSchoolCode: state.settings.neis.comciganSchoolCode || "69092",
    force,
  };
  try {
    const result = await window.schoolBoard.loadNeisDashboard(options);
    if (!result?.ok) throw new Error(result?.reason || "NEIS 자료를 불러오지 못했습니다.");
    neisData = result;
    neisError = result.offline ? result.staleReason || "실시간 연결 실패로 마지막 저장자료를 표시합니다." : "";
    if (result.school) {
      state.settings.neis.schoolName = result.school.schoolName || state.settings.neis.schoolName;
      state.settings.neis.officeCode = result.school.officeCode || state.settings.neis.officeCode;
      state.settings.neis.schoolCode = result.school.schoolCode || state.settings.neis.schoolCode;
      await save();
    }
  } catch (error) {
    neisError = error?.message || String(error);
    if (!neisData?.ok) neisData = null;
  } finally {
    neisLoading = false;
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    if (["dashboard", "timetable", "settings"].includes(state.activePage)) render(state.activePage);
  }
}

  state.settings.layoutLocked = !state.settings.layoutLocked;

}

const OPERATIONAL_RENDER_PAGES08172=new Set(['dashboard','attendance','students','alerts','work','duties','settings']);
function operationalPageNeedsRender08172(page){return OPERATIONAL_RENDER_PAGES08172.has(String(page||''));}
let operationalRefreshInFlight=null;
async function refreshOperationalCacheSilently({rerender=true}={}){
  if(operationalRefreshInFlight)return operationalRefreshInFlight;
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.previewOperationalSync)return refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender});
  operationalRefreshInFlight=(async()=>{
    if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok)return false;
    try{
      const result=await window.schoolBoard.previewOperationalSync();
      if(result?.requiresFullSync)return refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender});
      if(!result?.ok)throw new Error(result?.reason||'UEP 운영자료 갱신 실패');
      if(result.patch&&typeof result.patch==='object')readonlyCache={...(readonlyCache||{}),...result.patch,operationalSyncedAt:result.operationalSyncedAt||result.syncedAt||new Date().toISOString()};
      googleConnectionError='';updateTopSyncStatus();updateDormOutingCount();
      if(rerender&&state.activePage&&operationalPageNeedsRender08172(state.activePage))render(state.activePage);
      return true;
    }catch(error){googleConnectionError=error?.message||String(error);updateTopSyncStatus();return false;}
  })();
  try{return await operationalRefreshInFlight;}finally{operationalRefreshInFlight=null;}
}
function startReadonlyAutoRefresh(){
  if(readonlyAutoRefreshTimer) clearInterval(readonlyAutoRefreshTimer);
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok) return;
  readonlyAutoRefreshTimer=setInterval(()=>{
    if(document.hidden) return;
    readonlyAutoRefreshCycle+=1;
    const fullSyncedAt=readonlyCache?.syncedAt?new Date(readonlyCache.syncedAt).getTime():0;
    const fullAge=fullSyncedAt?Date.now()-fullSyncedAt:Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    const needsFull=!fullSyncedAt||fullAge>=READONLY_FULL_REFRESH_MAX_AGE_MS||readonlyAutoRefreshCycle%6===0;
    if(needsFull){
      const jitter=Math.floor(Math.random()*45000);
      setTimeout(()=>{if(!document.hidden)refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender:true});},jitter);
      return;
    }
    refreshOperationalCacheSilently({rerender:true});
  },READONLY_AUTO_REFRESH_MS);
}

function updateTopSyncStatus() {
  const button = $("#syncButton");
  if (!button) return;
  button.classList.remove("good", "warn", "bad");
  const label = button.querySelector("span");
  if (googleConnectionError) {
    button.classList.add("bad");
    if (label) label.textContent = "구글시트 오류";
    button.title = googleConnectionError;
    return;
  }
  if (readonlyCache?.students?.length && neisData?.ok && !neisData.offline) {
    button.classList.add("good");
    if (label) label.textContent = "동기화 정상";
    button.title = "UEP 구글시트와 NEIS 자료가 정상 연결되었습니다.";
    return;
  }
  if (readonlyCache?.students?.length) {
    button.classList.add("warn");
    if (label) label.textContent = neisData?.offline ? "NEIS 저장자료" : "NEIS 연결 대기";
    button.title = neisError || "UEP 구글시트는 연결되었고 NEIS 자료를 확인 중입니다.";
    return;
  }
  if (googleConnectionStatus?.ok) {
    button.classList.add("bad");
    if (label) label.textContent = "시트 권한 확인";
    button.title = googleConnectionError || "서비스 계정 이메일의 구글시트 공유 권한을 확인하세요.";
    return;
  }
  button.classList.add("warn");
  if (label) label.textContent = "인증 필요";
  button.title = "교사 이름·이메일 로그인과 기기승인 상태를 확인하세요.";
}

  if(currentRoleId()!=="admin")return toast("지각 기록 수정은 관리자만 가능합니다.");
  const students=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo||"").localeCompare(String(b.studentNo||"")));
  const selectedNo=String(row?.studentNo||"");
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent=row?"LATE ATTENDANCE · EDIT":"LATE ATTENDANCE · NEW";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent=row?"지각 기록 수정":"지각 기록 등록";
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=`<form id="lateAttendanceEditor" class="official-attendance-editor">
    <div class="official-editor-grid two"><label>일자<input type="date" id="lateDate" required value="${escapeHtml(row?.date||attendanceViewDate||dateKey(today))}"></label><label>학생<select id="lateStudent" required><option value="">학생 선택</option>${students.map(st=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(st.studentNo||"")}" ${String(st.studentNo)===selectedNo?"selected":""}>${escapeHtml(st.studentNo||"")} ${escapeHtml(st.name||"")} · ${escapeHtml(classNumberOf(st))}반</option>`).join("")}</select></label>
    <label>제출시각<input type="time" id="lateTime" value="${escapeHtml(row?.time||"")}"></label><label>지각시간(분)<input type="number" min="0" id="lateMinutes" value="${escapeHtml(row?.minutes||"")}"></label>
    <label class="span-two">사유<input id="lateReason" required value="${escapeHtml(row?.reason||"")}"></label><label>지도상태<select id="lateGuidance"><option ${row?.guidance==="일반"?"selected":""}>일반</option><option ${String(row?.guidance||"").includes("필요")?"selected":""}>지도필요</option><option ${row?.guidance==="지도완료"?"selected":""}>지도완료</option></select></label><label>비고<input id="lateNote" value="${escapeHtml(row?.note||"")}"></label></div>
    <div class="official-editor-actions">${row?'<button type="button" class="btn danger" id="lateDelete">삭제</button>':''}<button type="button" class="btn secondary" id="lateCancel">취소</button><button class="btn primary">${row?"수정 저장":"지각 등록"}</button></div></form>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");$("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");$("#lateCancel").onclick=closeDrawer;
  if(row&&$("#lateDelete"))$("#lateDelete").onclick=async()=>{await deleteLateAttendanceRow(row);closeDrawer();};
  $("#lateAttendanceEditor").onsubmit=async event=>{event.preventDefault();const student=students.find(st=>String(st.studentNo)===String($("#lateStudent").value));if(!student)return toast("학생을 선택하세요.");
    const previous=(readonlyCache?.lateAttendance||[]).filter(x=>String(x.studentId)===String(student.id)&&String(x.id)!==String(row?.id||""));
    const minutes=Number($("#lateMinutes").value||0), cumulativeCount=previous.length+1, cumulativeMinutes=previous.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+Number(x.minutes||0),0)+minutes;
    const payload={id:row?.id||"",studentId:student.id,studentNo:student.studentNo,name:student.name,grade:String(student.studentNo||"").slice(0,1),className:classNumberOf(student),date:$("#lateDate").value,time:$("#lateTime").value,reason:$("#lateReason").value.trim(),minutes,cumulativeCount,cumulativeMinutes,guidance:$("#lateGuidance").value,note:$("#lateNote").value.trim(),editor:currentLoginTeacherName()};
    try{const result=await window.schoolBoard.saveLateAttendance(payload);if(result?.patch)readonlyCache={...(readonlyCache||{}),...result.patch};attendanceViewDate=payload.date;closeDrawer();render("attendance");toast(row?"지각 기록을 수정했습니다.":"지각 기록을 등록했습니다.");}catch(error){toast(error?.message||"지각 저장에 실패했습니다.");}
  };
}
async function deleteLateAttendanceRow(row){
  if(currentRoleId()!=="admin"||!row?.id)return;
  if(!confirm(`${row.date} ${row.studentNo} ${row.name} 지각 기록을 삭제할까요?`))return;
  try{const result=await window.schoolBoard.deleteLateAttendance(row.id);if(result?.patch)readonlyCache={...(readonlyCache||{}),...result.patch};render("attendance");toast("지각 기록을 삭제했습니다.");}catch(error){toast(error?.message||"지각 삭제에 실패했습니다.");}
}

function attendancePersonalStatisticsView(officialRows,lateRows,nightRows) {
  const students=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo||"").localeCompare(String(b.studentNo||"")));
  if(attendanceStatStudentFilter!=="all"&&!students.some(s=>attendanceStudentKey(s)===attendanceStatStudentFilter))attendanceStatStudentFilter="all";
  const selectedStudent=attendanceStatStudentFilter==="all"?null:students.find(s=>attendanceStudentKey(s)===attendanceStatStudentFilter);
  const filterRows=rows=>selectedStudent?rows.filter(row=>attendanceStudentKey(row)===attendanceStudentKey(selectedStudent)):rows;
  const official=filterRows(officialRows).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)));
  const late=filterRows(lateRows).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)));
  const night=filterRows(nightRows).filter(row=>{
    const sourceText=`${row.result||""} ${row.type||""} ${row.affiliation||""} ${row.detail||""}`;
    const dormOnly=/학사/.test(sourceText)&&!/방과후|야간심화|공동교육과정|프로그램|출석|지각/.test(sourceText);
    return !dormOnly;
  }).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)));
  const dates=rows=>[...new Set(rows.map(x=>x.date).filter(Boolean))].sort().reverse();
  const classes=[...new Set(students.map(s=>String(classNumberOf(s)||'')).filter(Boolean))].sort((a,b)=>Number(a)-Number(b));
  if(selectedStudent) attendanceStatClassFilter=String(classNumberOf(selectedStudent)||attendanceStatClassFilter||'all');
  const scopedStudents=attendanceStatClassFilter==='all'?students:students.filter(s=>String(classNumberOf(s))===String(attendanceStatClassFilter));
  const classOptions=`<option value="all">전체 반</option>${classes.map(c=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(c)}" ${String(attendanceStatClassFilter)===String(c)?'selected':''}>${escapeHtml(c)}반</option>`).join('')}`;
  const studentOptions=scopedStudents.map(s=>`<option value="${escapeHtml(attendanceStudentKey(s))}" ${attendanceStatStudentFilter===attendanceStudentKey(s)?"selected":""}>${escapeHtml(s.studentNo||"")} ${escapeHtml(s.name||"")}</option>`).join("");
  const metricCard=(label,rows,detail)=>`<article class="attendance-personal-metric"><small>${label}</small><b>${rows.length}<em>건</em></b><span>${detail(rows)}</span></article>`;
  const history=(label,rows,value)=>`<section class="attendance-personal-history"><header><h3>${label}</h3><span>${rows.length}건 · ${dates(rows).length}일</span></header>${rows.length?rows.slice(0,60).map(row=>`<article><time>${escapeHtml(row.date||"-")}</time><b>${escapeHtml(value(row))}</b><p>${escapeHtml(row.reason||row.detail||row.result||row.programTitle||"")}</p></article>`).join(""):'<div class="empty-inline">기록이 없습니다.</div>'}</section>`;
  return `<div class="attendance-stat-page"><section class="attendance-stat-selector"><div><small>ATTENDANCE · PERSONAL INSIGHT</small><h3>${selectedStudent?`${escapeHtml(selectedStudent.studentNo)} ${escapeHtml(selectedStudent.name)}`:"학생 개인 출결 현황판"}</h3><p>반별 비교 없이 학생 한 명의 공결·지각·야자출결 누적 기록과 날짜를 확인합니다.</p></div><div class="attendance-stat-pickers"><label>반 선택<select id="attendanceStatClassFilter">${classOptions}</select></label><label>학생 선택<select id="attendanceStatStudentFilter"><option value="all">학생을 선택하세요</option>${studentOptions}</select></label></div></section>${selectedStudent?`<div class="attendance-personal-metrics">${metricCard("공결",official,r=>`${dates(r).length}일 · 최근 ${dates(r)[0]||"-"}`)}${metricCard("지각",late,r=>`${dates(r).length}일 · 최근 ${dates(r)[0]||"-"}`)}${metricCard("야자출결",night,r=>`${dates(r).length}일 · 학사 제외 · 실제 출석·프로그램만`)}</div><div class="attendance-personal-history-grid">${history("공결 기록",official,row=>`${row.type||"공결"} · ${row.period||"하루 전체"}`)}${history("지각 기록",late,row=>`${row.time||row.period||"지각"}`)}${history("야자출결 기록",night,row=>`${row.result||row.type||row.detail||"출결"}`)}</div>`:'<div class="query-empty attendance-stat-empty"><b>학생을 선택하면 개인 출결 통계가 표시됩니다.</b><small>공결 횟수와 날짜, 지각 횟수와 날짜, 야자출결 누적 현황을 한 화면에서 확인합니다.</small></div>'}</div>`;
}

      if(p.year&&p.month&&p.day)return p.year+'-'+p.mon

function uepComparableDate(value,basis=attendanceViewDate||dateKey(today)){
  if(value==null||value==='')return '';
  if(typeof value==='object')value=value.formattedValue||value.value||value.date||value.serial||'';
  const text=String(value).trim();
  // __UEP_08160_ISO_CALENDAR_DATE__
  if(/^20\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{2}T.*(?:Z|[+-]\d{2}:?\d{2})$/i.test(text)){
    const instant=new Date(text);
    if(!Number.isNaN(instant.getTime())){
      const parts=new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA',{timeZone:'Asia/Seoul',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'}).formatToParts(instant);
      const p=Object.fromEntries(parts.map(part=>[part.type,part.value]));
      if(p.year&&p.month&&p.day)return p.year+'-'+p.month+'-'+p.day;
    }
  }
  if(/^\d{5}(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(text)){
    const serial=Number(text),utc=new Date(Math.round((serial-25569)*86400000));
    if(!Number.isNaN(utc.getTime()))return `${utc.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(utc.getUTCMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(utc.getUTCDate()).padStart(2,'0')}`;
  }
  const full=text.match(/(20\d{2})[^0-9]+(\d{1,2})[^0-9]+(\d{1,2})/);
  if(full)return `${full[1]}-${full[2].padStart(2,'0')}-${full[3].padStart(2,'0')}`;
  const compact=text.replace(/\
===== readonlyCache={ @ 244577 =====
w.split('\n').filter(x=>x.trim());
  const studentLines=lines.filter(line=>/(?:^|[^0-9])[123][0-9]{3}(?![0-9])/.test(line));
  const drafts=(studentLines.length>=2 && studentLines.length>=Math.min(3,Math.ceil(lines.length*.25)))?studentLines.map(line=>inputCenterDraftFromPayload({...file,name:file.name},line)):[inputCenterDraftFromPayload(file,raw)];
  drafts.forEach(d=>{d.detectedType=classification.type;d.detectedConfidence=classification.confidence;d.detectedReason=classification.reason;});return drafts;
}
function inputCenterBundleDuplex(files=[]){
  if(({'Excel·CSV':'교사 파일','문서 파일':'교사 파일'}[inputCenterConfig.source]||inputCenterConfig.source)!=='수기 스캔'||!['양면 스캔','단면 스캔','사진(JPG/PNG)'].includes(inputCenterConfig.subtype)) return files.flatMap(file=>inputCenterExpandTextFile(file));
  const per=Math.max(1,Number(inputCenterConfig.pagesPerStudent|| (inputCenterConfig.subtype==='양면 스캔'?2:1)));
  const out=[];
  for(let i=0;i<files.length;i+=per){
    const pair=files.slice(i,i+per); const first=pair[0]||{};
    const text=pair.map(x=>String(x.text||'')).filter(Boolean).join('\n\n');
    const draft=inputCenterDraftFromPayload(first,text);
    draft.bundleId=`BUNDLE-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)}`;
    draft.pages=pair.map((x,j)=>({page:j+1,filePath:x.path||'',fileName:x.name||'',extension:x.extension||'',size:Number(x.size||0),rotation:0}));
    draft.pageCount=pair.length; draft.fileName=pair.map(x=>x.name).filter(Boolean).join(' + ')||(first.name||'스캔 원본');
    draft.matchStatus=draft.studentNo?draft.matchStatus:'판독 대기';
    draft.bundleStatus=pair.length===per?`${per}페이지 묶음 · 순서/회전 확인`:`예상 ${per}페이지 중 ${pair.length}페이지 · 확인 필요`;
    draft.batchScope=inputCenterConfig.batchScope; draft.batchGrade=inputCenterConfig.batchGrade; draft.batchClass=inputCenterConfig.batchClass;
    out.push(draft);
  }
  return out;
}
function inputCenterIsCompleted(row={}){return /정규화완료|적용완료/.test(String(row.status||row.processingStatus||''));}
async function refreshInputCenterSourceSilently(){
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.refreshInputCenterSource)return false;
  try{const result=await window.schoolBoard.refreshInputCenterSource();if(!result?.ok)return false;readonlyCache={...(readonlyCache||{}),externalInputs:result.externalInputs||[],inputCenterSyncedAt:result.inputCenterSyncedAt||new Date().toISOString()};return true;}catch{return false;}
}
function inputCenterAllHistory(){return (readonlyCache?.externalInputs||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(b.createdAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.createdAt||'')));}
function inputCenterHistory(){const rows=inputCenterAllHistory();return inputCenterConfig.historyMode==='completed'?rows.filter(inputCenterIsCompleted):rows.filter(x=>!inputCenterIsCompleted(x));}
function inputCenterLibraryRows(){
  const remote=inputCenterHistory().map(x=>({...x,storageMode:x.storageMode||'uep'}));
  // 02 검수·분류와 03 UEP 적용은 [UEP]입력센터 원본을 단일 기준으로 사용합니다.
  // 예전 PC 로컬 보관 이력은 새 정규화 파이프라인에 섞지 않습니다.
  if(inputCenterConfig.libraryScope!=='local') return remote.sort((a,b)=>String(b.createdAt||b.savedAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.createdAt||a.savedAt||'')));
  return (inputCenterLocalHistory||[]).map(x=>({...x,storageMode:'local'})).sort((a,b)=>String(b.createdAt||b.savedAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.createdAt||a.savedAt||'')));
}
function inputCenterProgramSemester(p={}){
  const text=`${p.semester||''} ${p.term||''} ${p.schoolTerm||''} ${p.date||''} ${p.startDate||''}`;
  if(/2학기|(^|[^0-9])2([^0-9]|$)/.test(text)) return '2';
  if(/1학기|(^|[^0-9])1([^0-9]|$)/.test(text)) return '1';
  const m=text.match(/2026[-/.](\d{1,2})/); if(m) return Number(m[1])<=7?'1':'2';
  return '';
}
function inputCenterProgramGroup(p={}){
  const text=`${p.kind||''} ${p.type||''} ${p.afterType||''} ${p.actualTitle||''} ${p.title||''}`;
  if(p.kind==='after'||/방과후|야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)) return /야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)?'night':'after';
  if(p.kind==='common'||/공통/.test(text)) return 'common';
  if(p.kind==='selected'||/선택/.test(text)) return 'selected';
  return '';
}
function inputCenterProgramIdentity(p={}){
  const group=inputCenterProgramGroup(p);
  const title=String(p.actualTitle||p.title||p.name||'').normalize('NFKC').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ');
  const teacher=String(p.teacher||p.teacherName||p.instructor||'').normalize('NFKC').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ');
  const semester=inputCenterProgramSemester(p);
  // 방과후/야간심화 readonly 데이터는 차시·출석 결합 과정에서 행별 임시 ID가 달라질 수 있습니다.
  // 연결목록은 강좌 마스터를 선택하는 곳이므로 학기+유형+강좌명+담당교사 기준으로 1회만 노출합니다.
  if(group==='after'||group==='night') return `${group}|${semester}|${title}|${teacher}`;
  const id=String(p.id||p.programId||p.afterId||p.afterSchoolId||p.activityId||p.masterId||'').trim();
  if(id) return `${group}|${id}`;
  return `${group}|${semester}|${title}|${teacher}`;
}
function inputCenterProgramCandidates(purpose=inputCenterConfig.purpose,semester=inputCenterConfig.semester){
  const filtered=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).filter(p=>{
    const group=inputCenterProgramGroup(p); if(!group) return false;
    const sem=inputCenterProgramSemester(p); if(sem&&semester&&sem!==String(semester)) return false;
    if(purpose==='보고서 연결') return (group==='common'||group==='selected') && p.reportRequired!==false && p.reportUse!==false;
    if(purpose==='프로그램 참가신청') return group==='selected'||group==='after'||group==='night';
    if(purpose==='만족도조사') return group==='common'||group==='selected'||group==='after'||group==='night';
    return false;
  });
  // readonly 프로그램 데이터에는 방과후 차시/일정 결합 때문에 같은 마스터 강좌가 여러 행으로 들어올 수 있습니다.
  // 입력센터 연결목록은 차시가 아니라 '프로그램 마스터 1건'을 선택하는 곳이므로 안정 ID 기준으로 1회만 노출합니다.
  const deduped=[]; const seen=new Set();
  filtered.forEach(p=>{const key=inputCenterProgramIdentity(p);if(seen.has(key))return;seen.add(key);deduped.push(p);});
  const rank={common:1,selected:2,after:3,night:4};
  return deduped.sort((a,b)=>(rank[inputCenterProgramGroup(a)]||9)-(rank[inputCenterProgramGroup(b)]||9)||String(a.actualTitle||a.title||'').localeCompare(String(b.actualTitle||b.title||'')));
}
function inputCenterProgramLabel(p={}){
  const g=inputCenterProgramGroup(p); const prefix={common:'공통',selected:'선택',after:'방과후',night:'야간심화'}[g]||'프로그램';
  const title=p.actualTitle||p.title||p.name||'';
  return `${prefix} · ${title}`;
}

function inputCenterHumanReportText(value=''){
  const source=String(value||'').trim();if(!source)return '';
  try{
    const meta=JSON.parse(source);
    const fields=Array.isArray(meta?.fields)?meta.fields:Array.isArray(meta)?meta:[];
    const skip=/^(연락처|전화번호|휴대폰|서명|이메일|학번|성명|이름|학년|반|수정시간|제출시간|제출일시)$/;
    const pairs=fields.map((f,i)=>({label:String(f?.label||`문항 ${i+1}`).trim(),value:String(f?.value||'').trim()})).filter(x=>x.value&&!skip.test(x.label));
    if(pairs.length)return pairs.map(x=>`[${x.label||'문항'}]\n${x.value}`).join('\n\n');
  }catch{}
  return source;
}
function inputCenterReviewDisplayText(row={}){
  const reviewed=inputCenterHumanReportText(row.reviewText||'');
  if(reviewed)return reviewed;
  const structured=inputCenterHumanReportText(row.normalizedText||'');
  if(structured&&!/^\s*[\[{]/.test(structured))return structured;
  if(structured&&structured!==String(row.normalizedText||'').trim())return structured;
  return String(row.rawText||row.text||'').trim();
}

  inputCenterDetail=row;
  const rawPaths=Array.isArray(row.pages)&&row.pages.length?row.pages.map(p=>p.filePath||p.storedPath||'').filter(Boolean):String(row.storedPath||row.filePath||'').split('|').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const assets=[];
  for(const p of rawPaths){const r=await window.schoolBoard?.readInputAsset?.(p);if(r?.ok)assets.push({...r,path:p,rotation:0});}
  const text=inputCenterReviewDisplayText(row);
  const raw=inputCenterHumanReportText(row.rawText||row.text||row.normalizedText||'');
  const body=`<div class="input-detail"><div class="input-detail-original"><div class="section-head"><div><small>ORIGINAL · ${assets.length||rawPaths.length}페이지</small><h3>원본 확인</h3></div></div>${assets.length?`<div class="input-page-tabs">${assets.map((a,i)=>`<button class="${i===0?'active':''}" data-input-page="${i}">${i+1}페이지</button>`).join('')}</div><div class="input-page-view">${assets.map((a,i)=>`<div class="input-page ${i===0?'active':''}" data-input-page-panel="${i}"><img src="${a.dataUrl}" data-input-page-img="${i}" alt="${i+1}페이지 원본"><div><button class="btn secondary" data-input-rotate="${i}">↻ 90° 회전</button><button class="btn secondary" data-input-open-asset="${i}">원본 파일 열기</button></div></div>`).join('')}</div>`:`<div class="empty-state"><b>미리 볼 수 있는 이미지 원본이 없습니다.</b><span>PDF 등은 원본 파일 열기로 확인할 수 있습니다.</span></div>`}</div><div class="input-detail-text"><small>TEXT REVIEW · 원본과 검수본 분리 보존</small><h3>${escapeHtml(row.studentNo||'학생 확인 전')} ${escapeHtml(row.name||'')}</h3><div class="input-ocr-meta"><span>${escapeHtml(row.ocrStatus||'OCR 상태 미확인')}</span><span>${escapeHtml(row.ocrEngine||'로컬 OCR')}</span>${row.ocrError?`<span class="warn">${escapeHtml(row.ocrError)}</span>`:''}</div><label>원본에서 읽은 내용<textarea rows="12" readonly>${escapeHtml(raw||'원본에서 읽은 내용이 없습니다.   $('#drawerKicker').textContent=`${row.source||'입력센터'} · ${row.title||row.fileName||'학생 자료'}`;$('#drawerTitle').textContent='자료 상세보기';$('#drawerBody').innerHTML=body;$('#drawer').classList.remove('hidden');$('#drawerBackdrop').classList.remove('hidden');
  $$('[data-input-page]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{$$('[data-input-page]').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x===b));$$('[data-input-page-panel]').forEach(x=>x.classList.toggle('active',x.dataset.inputPagePanel===b.dataset.inputPage));});

function inputCenterSuggestTarget(rows=[]){

function bindInputCenter(){
  $$('[data-input-tab]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{inputCenterConfig.tab=b.dataset.inputTab;render('inputs');});
  $$('[data-input-history-mode]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{inputCenterConfig.historyMode=b.dataset.inputHistoryMode||'active';inputCenterConfig.batchFilter='latest';render('inputs');});
  $('[data-input-batch-filter]')?.addEventListener('change',e=>{inputCenterConfig.batchFilter=e.target.value;render('inputs');});
  $$('[data-input-scope]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{inputCenterConfig.libraryScope=b.dataset.inputScope;render('inputs');});
  $$('[data-input-grade-filter]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{inputCenterConfig.gradeFilter=b.dataset.inputGradeFilter;inputCenterConfig.classFilter='all';render('inputs');});
  $$('[data-input-class-filter]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{inputCenterConfig.classFilter=b.dataset.inputClassFilter;render('inputs');});
  $$('[data-input-match-filter]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{inputCenterConfig.matchFilter=b.dataset.inputMatchFilter;render('inputs');});
  $$('[data-input-source-filter]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{inputCenterConfig.sourceFilter=b.dataset.inputSourceFilter;render('inputs');});
  $$('[data-input-view]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const rows=inputCenterLibraryRows().filter(x=>inputCenterConfig.libraryScope==='local'?x.storageMode==='local':inputCenterConfig.libraryScope==='uep'?x.storageMode!=='local':true);const row=rows[Number(b.dataset.inputView)];if(row)inputCenterOpenDetail(row);});

  $('[data-input-confirm-classification]')?.addEventListener('click',async()=>{const rows=filtered;if(!rows.length)return toast('확정할 배치가 없습니다.');const nature=$('[data-input-review-nature]')?.value||'기타';const programId=$('[data-input-review-program]')?.value||'';const program=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).find(v=>String(v.id||v.programId||'')===String(programId))||{};const result=await window.schoolBoard?.classifyInputBatch?.({batchId:activeBatchId,nature,programId,programKind:program.kind||'',programName:program.actualTitle||program.title||'',programSemester:program.semester||program.term||''});if(!result?.ok)return toast(result?.reason||'검수·분류 저장에 실패했습니다.');if(Array.isArray(result?.externalInputs))readonlyCache={...(readonlyCache||{}),externalInputs:result.externalInputs,inputCenterSyncedAt:result.inputCenterSyncedAt||new Date().toISOString()};else await refreshInputCenterSourceSilently();inputCenterConfig.tab='review';toast(`${result.count||rows.length}건 검수·분류 확정 · 03 UEP 적용으로 이동합니다.`);render('inputs');});

function submissionDateText(value){
  const raw=String(value??"").trim();
  if(!raw)return "-";
  const serial=Number(raw);
  let date=null;
  if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000)date=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+serial*86400000);
  else{
    const parsed=Date.parse(raw.replace(/오전/g,"AM").replace(/오후/g,"PM"));
    if(Number.isFinite(parsed))date=new Date(parsed);
  }
  if(!date||Number.isNaN(date.getTime()))return raw;
  return `${date.getFullYear()}.${String(date.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")}.${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2,"0")}`;
}
function reportSubmissionLabel(report){
  const date=submissionDateText(report?.finalSubmittedAt||report?.submittedAt);
  const count=Math.max(1,Number(report?.submissionCount)||1);
  return `${date} (${count}차 제출)`;
}

let uepSchoolReadDiagnostics = [];
function uepDiagSafe(value,depth=0){
  if(depth>6)return '[DEPTH_LIMIT]';
  if(value==null||typeof value==='number'||typeof value==='boolean')return value;
  if(typeof value==='string')return value.replace(/[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}/gi,'[EMAIL_REDACTED]').slice(0,4000);
  if(Array.isArray(value))return value.slice(0,30).map(v=>uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1));
  if(typeof value==='object'){
    const out={};
    for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(value)){
      if(/token|secret|password|authorization|cookie|credential|private|email|name/i.test(k)){out[k]='[REDACTED]';continue;}
      out[k]=uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1);
    }
    return out;
  }
  return String(value);
}
function uepDiagRecord(stage,value){
  uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.push({at:new Date().toISOString(),stage,value:uepDiagSafe(value)});
  if(uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.length>80)uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.shift();
}
async function downloadSchoolReadDiagnostics(){
  let verified=null;
  try{verified=await window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus?.({verify:true});uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify',verified);}
  catch(error){uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});}
  const report={
    schema:'uep-school-read-diagnostic-v1',
    appVersion:APP_VERSION,
    exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
    platform:navigator.platform,
    online:navigator.onLine,
    schoolReadBridge:{
      sessionStatus:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus),
      login:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadLogin),
      preview:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync),
      readCache:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache)
    },
    googleConnectionStatus:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionStatus),
    googleConnectionError:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionError),
    cacheSummary:{
      syncedAt:readonlyCache?.syncedAt||null,
      students:readonlyCache?.students?.length||0,
      dormStudents:readonlyCache?.dormStudents?.length||0
    },
    verifiedSession:uepDiagSafe(verified),
    events:uepSchoolReadDiagnostics
  };
  const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(report,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url;
  a.download='UEP-school-read-diagnostic-'+new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g,'-')+'.json';
  document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),2000);
  toast('진단 JSON 파일을 저장했습니다. 이 파일을 채팅에 올려 주세요.');
}

async function selectGoogleCredential() {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.selectGoogleCredential)
    return openOAuthGuide();
  const result = await window.schoolBoard.selectGoogleCredential();
  if (result?.canceled) return;
  if (!result?.ok) { googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "Google 서비스 계정 인증을 연결하지 못했습니다."; updateTopSyncStatus(); return toast(googleConnectionError); }
  googleConnectionError = "";
  googleConnectionStatus = await window.schoolBoard.googleCredentialStatus();
  const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
  if (cached?.ok) { readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data); googleConnectionError = ""; }
  else googleConnectionError = cached?.reason || "UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.";
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  startReadonlyAutoRefresh();
  toast(`${result.account || "Google 서비스 계정"}으로 UEP 구글시트에 연결했습니다.`);
  render("settings");
}

async function previewReadonlySync() {
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync)return toast("학교 공용 데이터 연결 기능을 찾지 못했습니다.");
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok){
    const status=await ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify:true}).catch(()=>null);
    googleConnectionStatus={ok:Boolean(status?.authenticated),encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api',endpointReady:status?.endpointReady!==false};
  }
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok)return toast("학교 공용 읽기 연결에 실패했습니다. 로그아웃 후 이름·이메일로 다시 로그인해 주세요.");

  catch(error){
    uepDiagRecord('sync.preview.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});
    result={ok:false,reason:error?.message||'학교 공용 자료 조회 예외'};
  }
  if (!result?.ok) {
    if (button) {
      button.disabled = false;
      button.textContent = "지금 새로고침";
    }
    googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "동기화하지 못했습니다.";
    updateTopSyncStatus();

  }
  googleConnectionError = "";
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  const p = result.preview;
  const n = result.nightPreview || {};
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "GOOGLE SHEETS";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent = "UEP 구글시트 조회 결과";
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="sync-preview"><div><b>${p.masterCount}</b><span>현재원</span></div><div><b>${p.nfcCount ?? "-"}</b><span>NFC 등록</span></div><div><b>${p.dormRosterCount ?? "-"}</b><span>학사외출</span></div><div><b>${n.attended ?? "-"}</b><span>야자 출석행</span></div><div class="${n.outsideQr ? "warn" : ""}"><b>${n.outsideQr ??   $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");

async function retryGoogleConnection() {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync) return navigate("settings");
  toast("UEP 구글시트를 다시 읽는 중입니다.");
  const result = await window.schoolBoard.previewReadonlySync();
  if (!result?.ok) {
    googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "UEP 구글시트 연결에 실패했습니다.";
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    toast(googleConnectionError);
    render("dashboard");
    return;
  }
  const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
  if (cached?.ok) {
    readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data);
    googleConnectionError = "";
    toast("UEP 구글시트를 다시 불러왔습니다.");
  } else {
    googleConnectionError = cached?.reason || "UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.";
    toast(googleConnectionError);
  }
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  render("dashboard");
}

function updateConnectionControls() {
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  const connected = Boolean(readonlyCache?.students?.length && !googleConnectionError);
  const status = $(".connection-title .state");
  if (status && connected) {
    status.textContent = "구글시트 연결됨";
    status.classList.remove("warn", "error");
  }
  const sync = $("[data-readonly-sync]");
  if (sync) sync.disabled = !googleConnectionStatus?.ok;
}

function updateDashboardTimetableControls() {
  const card = $(".timetable-card");
  if (!card) return;
  card.querySelectorAll(".timetable-popup-actions").forEach(node=>node.remove());
}

function shiftTimetableDate(value, amountDays) {
  const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return dateKey(new Date());
  base.setDate(base.getDate() + Number(amountDays || 0));
  return dateKey(base);
}
function timetableWeekLabel(value) {
  const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return "주간 선택";
  const day = base.getDay();
  base.setDate(base.getDate() + (day === 0 ? -6 : 1 - day));
  const end = new Date(base); end.setDate(end.getDate() + 4);
  const md = d => `${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()}`;
  return `${md(base)} ~ ${md(end)}`;
}

    const needsFull=!readonlyCache||!fullSyncedAt||fullAge>=READONLY_FULL_REFRESH_MAX_AGE_MS;
    const jitter=needsFull?(2000+Math.floor(Math.random()*10000)):(500+Math.floor(Math.random()*2500));

    // 데이터 연결 단계에서 멈춘다. 학생정보 화면을 먼저 열어 권한부여를 건너뛰지 않는다.

function applyProgramWritePatch08172(target,patch){
  if(!target||!patch)return target;
  for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(patch)){if(value!==undefined)target[key]=value;}
  if(patch.commonReportFormUrl!==undefined)target.reportFormUrl=patch.commonReportFormUrl;
  if(patch.activityType!==undefined)target.type=patch.activityType;
  if(patch.nightLinked!==undefined)target.affectsAttendance=Boolean(patch.nightLinked);
  return target;
}

async function migrateAdminProgramOverridesToSheetOnce(){
  if(currentRoleId()!=="admin" || state.settings?.programSheetMigrationV07662 || !window.schoolBoard?.saveProgramOperation) return;
  const overrides=state.programOverrides&&typeof state.programOverrides==="object"?state.programOverrides:{};
  const programs=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.programs)?readonlyCache.programs:[];
  let changed=false, failed=false;
  for(const [id,override] of Object.entries(overrides)){
    if(!override || !["date","time","place","teacher"].some(key=>String(override[key]??"").trim())) continue;
    const program=programs.find(item=>String(item.id||"")===String(id));
    if(!program?.source) continue;
    try{
      const result=await window.schoolBoard.saveProgramOperation({role:"admin",source:program.source,programId:program.programId||program.courseId||"",sessionId:program.sessionId||"",date:override.date??program.date??"",time:override.time??program.time??"",place:override.place??program.place??"",teacher:override.teacher??program.teacher??""});
      if(!result?.ok) throw new Error(result?.reason||"시트 저장 실패");
      if(result?.programPatch)applyProgramWritePatch08172(program,result.programPatch);
      const kept={...override}; ["date","time","place","teacher"].forEach(key=>delete kept[key]);
      if(Object.keys(kept).length) state.programOverrides[id]=kept; else delete state.programOverrides[id];
      changed=true;
    }catch(error){ failed=true; console.warn("[UEP] 프로그램 로컬값 시트 이관 실패",id,error); }
  }
  if(!failed){ state.settings.programSheetMigrationV07662=true; changed=true; }
  if(changed) await save();
}

let readonlyRefreshInFlight=null;
async function refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force=true, rerender=true}={}) {
  if(readonlyRefreshInFlight)return readonlyRefreshInFlight;
  readonlyRefreshInFlight=(async()=>{
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync || !window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache)return false;
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok){
    const status=await ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify:true}).catch(()=>null);
    googleConnectionStatus={ok:Boolean(status?.authenticated),encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api',endpointReady:status?.endpointReady!==false};
  }
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok)return false;
  try {
    if(force){
      const sync=await window.schoolBoard.previewReadonlySync();
      if(!sync?.ok) throw new Error(sync?.reason||'UEP 학교 데이터 자동 갱신 실패');
    }
    const cached=await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
    if(!cached?.ok) throw new Error(cached?.reason||'UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.');
    readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data);
    googleConnectionError='';
    if(typeof studentBadgeCache!=='undefined'&&studentBadgeCache?.clear) studentBadgeCache.clear();
    updateDormOutingCount();
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    if(rerender && !setupWizardActive){
      const scrollY=window.scrollY;
      render(state.activePage||'dashboard');
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.scrollTo(0,scrollY));
    }
    return true;
  } catch(error) {
    googleConnectionError=error?.message||'UEP 학교 데이터 자동 갱신 실패';
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    return false;
  }
  })();
  try{return await readonlyRefreshInFlight;}finally{readonlyRefreshInFlight=null;}
}
const OPERATIONAL_RENDER_PAGES08172=new Set(['dashboard','attendance','students','alerts','work','duties','settings']);
function operationalPageNeedsRender08172(page){return OPERATIONAL_RENDER_PAGES08172.has(String(page||''));}
let operationalRefreshInFlight=null;
async function refreshOperationalCacheSilently({rerender=true}={}){
  if(operationalRefreshInFlight)return operationalRefreshInFlight;
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.previewOperationalSync)return refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender});
  operationalRefreshInFlight=(async()=>{
    if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok)return false;
    try{
      const result=await window.schoolBoard.previewOperationalSync();
      if(result?.requiresFullSync)return refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender});
      if(!result?.ok)throw new Error(result?.reason||'UEP 운영자료 갱신 실패');
      if(result.patch&&typeof result.patch==='object')readonlyCache={...(readonlyCache||{}),...result.patch,operationalSyncedAt:result.operationalSyncedAt||result.syncedAt||new Date().toISOString()};
      googleConnectionError='';updateTopSyncStatus();updateDormOutingCount();
      if(rerender&&state.activePage&&operationalPageNeedsRender08172(state.activePage))render(state.activePage);
      return true;
    }catch(error){googleConnectionError=error?.message||String(error);updateTopSyncStatus();return false;}
  })();
  try{return await operationalRefreshInFlight;}finally{operationalRefreshInFlight=null;}
}
function startReadonlyAutoRefresh(){
  if(readonlyAutoRefreshTimer) clearInterval(readonlyAutoRefreshTimer);
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok) return;
  readonlyAutoRefreshTimer=setInterval(()=>{
    if(document.hidden) return;
    readonlyAutoRefreshCycle+=1;
    const fullSyncedAt=readonlyCache?.syncedAt?new Date(readonlyCache.syncedAt).getTime():0;
    const fullAge=fullSyncedAt?Date.now()-fullSyncedAt:Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    const needsFull=!fullSyncedAt||fullAge>=READONLY_FULL_REFRESH_MAX_AGE_MS||readonlyAutoRefreshCycle%6===0;
    if(needsFull){
      const jitter=Math.floor(Math.random()*45000);
      setTimeout(()=>{if(!document.hidden)refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender:true});},jitter);
      return;
    }
    refreshOperationalCacheSilently({rerender:true});
  },READONLY_AUTO_REFRESH_MS);
}

function updateTopSyncStatus() {
  const button = $("#syncButton");
  if (!button) return;
  button.classList.remove("good", "warn", "bad");
  const label = button.querySelector("span");
  if (googleConnectionError) {
    button.classList.add("bad");
    if (label) label.textContent = "구글시트 오류";
    button.title = googleConnectionError;
    return;
  }
  if (readonlyCache?.students?.length && neisData?.ok && !neisData.offline) {
    button.classList.add("good");
    if (label) label.textContent = "동기화 정상";
    button.title = "UEP 구글시트와 NEIS 자료가 정상 연결되었습니다.";
    return;
  }
  if (readonlyCache?.students?.length) {
    button.classList.add("warn");
    if (label) label.textContent = neisData?.offline ? "NEIS 저장자료" : "NEIS 연결 대기";
    button.title = neisError || "UEP 구글시트는 연결되었고 NEIS 자료를 확인 중입니다.";
    return;
  }
  if (googleConnectionStatus?.ok) {
    button.classList.add("bad");
    if (label) label.textContent = "시트 권한 확인";
    button.title = googleConnectionError || "서비스 계정 이메일의 구글시트 공유 권한을 확인하세요.";
    return;
  }
  button.classList.add("warn");
  if (label) label.textContent = "인증 필요";
  button.title = "교사 이름·이메일 로그인과 기기승인 상태를 확인하세요.";
}

const UEP_08250_LOAD={core:'ready',academic:'waiting',admission:'waiting',running:false};
let UEP_08274_STAGE_REFRESH_PROMISE=null;
function uep08250Emit(){try{window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('uep:data-stage',{detail:{...UEP_08250_LOAD}}));}catch{}}
function uep08274AdmissionRowsReady(){return Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.universityAdmissions)&&readonlyCache.universityAdmissions.length>0;}
async function uep08250RefreshStage(stage){
  if(stage==='admission'&&uep08274AdmissionRowsReady()){UEP_08250_LOAD.admission='ready';uep08250Emit();return true;}
  if(UEP_08274_STAGE_REFRESH_PROMISE){await UEP_08274_STAGE_REFRESH_PROMISE;return stage!=='admission'||uep08274AdmissionRowsReady();}
  UEP_08250_LOAD[stage]='loading';uep08250Emit();
  UEP_08274_STAGE_REFRESH_PROMISE=(async()=>{
    try{
      const r=await window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync?.();
      if(!r?.ok)throw new Error(r?.reason||'UEP 자료 동기화 실패');
      const c=await window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache?.();
      if(!c?.ok||!c.data)throw new Error(c?.reason||'UEP 캐시를 읽지 못했습니다.');
      readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(c.data);googleConnectionError='';
      UEP_08250_LOAD.academic='ready';
      UEP_08250_LOAD.admission=uep08274AdmissionRowsReady()?'ready':'error';
      return true;
    }catch(e){
      if(UEP_08250_LOAD[stage]==='loading')UEP_08250_LOAD[stage]='error';
      googleConnectionError=e?.message||String(e);return false;
    }finally{UEP_08274_STAGE_REFRESH_PROMISE=null;uep08250Emit();}
  })();
  await UEP_08274_STAGE_REFRESH_PROMISE;
  return stage!=='admission'||uep08274AdmissionRowsReady();
}
async function uep08250StartBackgroundStages(){
  if(UEP_08250_LOAD.running)return;
  UEP_08250_LOAD.running=true;uep08250Emit();
  try{
    if(uep08274AdmissionRowsReady()){UEP_08250_LOAD.academic='ready';UEP_08250_LOAD.admission='ready';uep08250Emit();return;}
    await uep08250RefreshStage('admission');
  }finally{UEP_08250_LOAD.running=false;uep08250Emit();}
}

  if(uep08274AdmissionRowsReady()){UEP_08250_LOAD.admission='ready';uep08250Emit();return true;}
  await uep08250RefreshStage('admission');
  return uep08274AdmissionRowsReady();
}

async function initializeUserSessionGate(){
  const remembered=state?.auth?.rememberUser?state?.auth?.rememberedUser:null;
  const savedUser=state?.auth?.user;
  if(uep08125ValidTeacherName(remembered?.name)&&remembered?.email&&window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus){
    try{
      const status=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadSessionStatus({verify:true});
      if(status?.authenticated){
        const a=status.user||{};const account={id:String(a.userId||remembered?.id||savedUser?.id||''),email:String(a.email||remembered?.email||savedUser?.email||'').trim(),name:uep08125PickTeacherName(a.name,remembered?.name,savedUser?.name),role:String(a.role||''),admin:a.isAdmin?'Y':'N',grade:String(a.grade||'1'),classNo:String(a.homeroom||''),department:String(a.department||((a.grade||1)+'학년부')),active:true,activeRaw:'Y'};state.auth.user=accountToSession(account);state.settings.userProfile={...state.settings.userProfile,...state.auth.user};state.settings.loginUserName=state.auth.user.name;state.settings.teacherName=state.auth.user.name;googleConnectionStatus={ok:true,encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api'};
        await save();
        hideUserAuthGate();
        return true;
      }
    }catch(error){console.warn('[UEP] 저장된 School Read 세션 확인 실패',error);}
  }
  state.auth.user=null;
  authSessionReady=false;
  state.auth.locked=true;
  renderUserAuthGate({switchUser:true});
  authGateMessage('교사 이름과 이메일로 UEP를 시작하세요.');
  return false;
}

async function initializeUserSessionGate(){
  const remembered=state?.auth?.rememberUser?state?.auth?.rememberedUser:null;
  const savedUser=state?.auth?.user;
  if(uep08125ValidTeacherName(remembered?.name)&&remembered?.email&&window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus){
    try{
      const status=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadSessionStatus({verify:true});
      if(status?.authenticated){
        const a=status.user||{};const account={id:String(a.userId||remembered?.id||savedUser?.id||''),email:String(a.email||remembered?.email||savedUser?.email||'').trim(),name:uep08125PickTeacherName(a.name,remembered?.name,savedUser?.name),role:String(a.role||''),admin:a.isAdmin?'Y':'N',grade:String(a.grade||'1'),classNo:String(a.homeroom||''),department:String(a.department||((a.grade||1)+'학년부')),active:true,activeRaw:'Y'};state.auth.user=accountToSession(account);state.settings.userProfile={...state.settings.userProfile,...state.auth.user};state.settings.loginUserName=state.auth.user.name;state.settings.teacherName=state.auth.user.name;googleConnectionStatus={ok:true,encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api'};
        await save();
        hideUserAuthGate();
        return true;
      }
    }catch(error){console.warn('[UEP] 저장된 School Read 세션 확인 실패',error);}
  }
  state.auth.user=null;
  authSessionReady=false;
  state.auth.locked=true;
  renderUserAuthGate({switchUser:true});
  authGateMessage('교사 이름과 이메일로 UEP를 시작하세요.');
  return false;
}

  </div>`;
}

let uepSchoolReadDiagnostics = [];
function uepDiagSafe(value,depth=0){
  if(depth>6)return '[DEPTH_LIMIT]';
  if(value==null||typeof value==='number'||typeof value==='boolean')return value;
  if(typeof value==='string')return value.replace(/[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}/gi,'[EMAIL_REDACTED]').slice(0,4000);
  if(Array.isArray(value))return value.slice(0,30).map(v=>uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1));
  if(typeof value==='object'){
    const out={};
    for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(value)){
      if(/token|secret|password|authorization|cookie|credential|private|email|name/i.test(k)){out[k]='[REDACTED]';continue;}
      out[k]=uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1);
    }
    return out;
  }
  return String(value);
}
function uepDiagRecord(stage,value){
  uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.push({at:new Date().toISOString(),stage,value:uepDiagSafe(value)});
  if(uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.length>80)uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.shift();
}
async function downloadSchoolReadDiagnostics(){
  let verified=null;
  try{verified=await window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus?.({verify:true});uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify',verified);}
  catch(error){uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});}
  const report={
    schema:'uep-school-read-diagnostic-v1',
    appVersion:APP_VERSION,
    exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
    platform:navigator.platform,
    online:navigator.onLine,
    schoolReadBridge:{
      sessionStatus:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus),
      login:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadLogin),
      preview:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync),
      readCache:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache)
    },
    googleConnectionStatus:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionStatus),
    googleConnectionError:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionError),
    cacheSummary:{
      syncedAt:readonlyCache?.syncedAt||null,
      students:readonlyCache?.students?.length||0,
      dormStudents:readonlyCache?.dormStudents?.length||0
    },
    verifiedSession:uepDiagSafe(verified),
    events:uepSchoolReadDiagnostics
  };
  const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(report,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url;
  a.download='UEP-school-read-diagnostic-'+new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g,'-')+'.json';
  document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),2000);
  toast('진단 JSON 파일을 저장했습니다. 이 파일을 채팅에 올려 주세요.');
}

function applyProgramWritePatch08172(target,patch){
  if(!target||!patch)return target;
  for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(patch)){if(value!==undefined)target[key]=value;}
  if(patch.commonReportFormUrl!==undefined)target.reportFormUrl=patch.commonReportFormUrl;
  if(patch.activityType!==undefined)target.type=patch.activityType;
  if(patch.nightLinked!==undefined)target.affectsAttendance=Boolean(patch.nightLinked);
  return target;
}

async function migrateAdminProgramOverridesToSheetOnce(){
  if(currentRoleId()!=="admin" || state.settings?.programSheetMigrationV07662 || !window.schoolBoard?.saveProgramOperation) return;
  const overrides=state.programOverrides&&typeof state.programOverrides==="object"?state.programOverrides:{};
  const programs=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.programs)?readonlyCache.programs:[];
  let changed=false, failed=false;
  for(const [id,override] of Object.entries(overrides)){
    if(!override || !["date","time","place","teacher"].some(key=>String(override[key]??"").trim())) continue;
    const program=programs.find(item=>String(item.id||"")===String(id));
    if(!program?.source) continue;
    try{
      const result=await window.schoolBoard.saveProgramOperation({role:"admin",source:program.source,programId:program.programId||program.courseId||"",sessionId:program.sessionId||"",date:override.date??program.date??"",time:override.time??program.time??"",place:override.place??program.place??"",teacher:override.teacher??program.teacher??""});
      if(!result?.ok) throw new Error(result?.reason||"시트 저장 실패");
      if(result?.programPatch)applyProgramWritePatch08172(program,result.programPatch);
      const kept={...override}; ["date","time","place","teacher"].forEach(key=>delete kept[key]);
      if(Object.keys(kept).length) state.programOverrides[id]=kept; else delete state.programOverrides[id];
      changed=true;
    }catch(error){ failed=true; console.warn("[UEP] 프로그램 로컬값 시트 이관 실패",id,error); }
  }
  if(!failed){ state.settings.programSheetMigrationV07662=true; changed=true; }
  if(changed) await save();
}

  try{status=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadSessionStatus({verify:true});uepDiagRecord('session.status.after',status);}
  catch(error){uepDiagRecord('session.status.after.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});return {authenticated:false,reason:error?.message||'로그인 후 세션 검증 예외'};}
  return status||{authenticated:false};
}

  const credentialTask = window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus
    ? withStartupTimeout(ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify:true}), 8000, null)
        .then((status) => {
          if(status) googleConnectionStatus={ok:Boolean(status.authenticated),encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api',endpointReady:status.endpointReady!==false};
          if(!status?.authenticated && status?.reason) googleConnectionError=status.reason;
        })
        .catch((error) => {googleConnectionStatus={ok:false,encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api'};googleConnectionError=error?.message||'학교 공용 읽기 연결 실패';console.warn("[UEP] School Read API 최초 연결 실패", error);})
    : Promise.resolve();
  const cacheTask = window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache
    ? withStartupTimeout(window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache(), 8000, null)
        .then((cached) => {
          if (cached?.ok) { readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data); googleConnectionError = ""; }
          else if (cached) googleConnectionError = cached.reason || "학교 데이터 동기화를 확인하세요. 마지막 저장 자료는 계속 사용할 수 있습니다.";
        })
        .catch((error) => {
          console.warn("[UEP] 읽기 캐시 불러오기 실패", error);
          googleConnectionError = error?.message || "학교 데이터 동기화를 확인하세요. 마지막 저장 자료는 계속 사용할 수 있습니다.";
        })
    : Promise.resolve();
  await Promise.all([credentialTask, cacheTask]);
  // 화면은 로컬/메모리 캐시로 먼저 표시한 뒤, 실행당 한 번만 백그라운드 원격 동기화한다.
  // 사용자는 기다리지 않고 UEP를 사용하며, 최신 Google Sheet가 도착하면 현재 화면만 갱신한다.
  if(googleConnectionStatus?.ok){
    const fullSyncedAt=readonlyCache?.syncedAt?new Date(readonlyCache.syncedAt).getTime():0;
    const fullAge=fullSyncedAt?Date.now()-fullSyncedAt:Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
    const needsFull=!readonlyCache||!fullSyncedAt||fullAge>=READONLY_FULL_REFRESH_MAX_AGE_MS;
    const jitter=needsFull?(2000+Math.floor(Math.random()*10000)):(500+Math.floor(Math.random()*2500));
    setTimeout(async()=>{
      const ok=needsFull
        ? await refreshReadonlyCacheSilently({force:true,rerender:true})
        : await refreshOperationalCacheSilently({rerender:true});
      if(ok){
        updateTopSyncStatus();
        if(currentRoleId()==='admin') migrateAdminProgramOverridesToSheetOnce().catch(()=>{});
      }
    },jitter);
  }
  if(googleConnectionStatus?.ok && readonlyCache) await migrateAdminProgramOverridesToSheetOnce();
  updateDormOutingCount();
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  startReadonlyAutoRefresh();
  // 여기서는 백그라운드에서 최신 계정 유효성을 재검증한다.
  // 자동로그인 세션은 이미 화면을 열었으므로 네트워크 지연이 시작 화면을 막지 않는다.
  await Promise.race([
    initializeUserSessionGate(),
    new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(()=>{
      try{
        /* 0.81.20: local remembered state never bypasses School Read session verification */
      }catch{}
      resolve(true);
    },1800))
  ]);
  if(authSessionReady && !resumedRememberedSession) navigate(state.activePage || "dashboard");
  if(false && !googleConnectionStatus?.ok && currentRoleId()==="admin"){
    // 인증정보가 없거나 복호화/유효성 검증에 실패한 경우에는 기존 setupCompleted 값과 무관하게
    // 데이터 연결 단계에서 멈춘다. 학생정보 화면을 먼저 열어 권한부여를 건너뛰지 않는다.
    setTimeout(()=>openSetupWizard(3),50);
  }else if(!state.settings.setupCompleted){
    setTimeout(()=>openSetupWizard(0),50);
  }else{
    setSetupWizardLock(false);
  }
}

function applyProgramWritePatch08172(target,patch){
  if(!target||!patch)return target;
  for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(patch)){if(value!==undefined)target[key]=value;}
  if(patch.commonReportFormUrl!==undefined)target.reportFormUrl=patch.commonReportFormUrl;
  if(patch.activityType!==undefined)target.type=patch.activityType;
  if(patch.nightLinked!==undefined)target.affectsAttendance=Boolean(patch.nightLinked);
  return target;
}

async function migrateAdminProgramOverridesToSheetOnce(){
  if(currentRoleId()!=="admin" || state.settings?.programSheetMigrationV07662 || !window.schoolBoard?.saveProgramOperation) return;
  const overrides=state.programOverrides&&typeof state.programOverrides==="object"?state.programOverrides:{};
  const programs=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.programs)?readonlyCache.programs:[];
  let changed=false, failed=false;
  for(const [id,override] of Object.entries(overrides)){
    if(!override || !["date","time","place","teacher"].some(key=>String(override[key]??"").trim())) continue;
    const program=programs.find(item=>String(item.id||"")===String(id));
    if(!program?.source) continue;
    try{
      const result=await window.schoolBoard.saveProgramOperation({role:"admin",source:program.source,programId:program.programId||program.courseId||"",sessionId:program.sessionId||"",date:override.date??program.date??"",time:override.time??program.time??"",place:override.place??program.place??"",teacher:override.teacher??program.teacher??""});
      if(!result?.ok) throw new Error(result?.reason||"시트 저장 실패");
      if(result?.programPatch)applyProgramWritePatch08172(program,result.programPatch);
      const kept={...override}; ["date","time","place","teacher"].forEach(key=>delete kept[key]);
      if(Object.keys(kept).length) state.programOverrides[id]=kept; else delete state.programOverrides[id];
      changed=true;
    }catch(error){ failed=true; console.warn("[UEP] 프로그램 로컬값 시트 이관 실패",id,error); }
  }
  if(!failed){ state.settings.programSheetMigrationV07662=true; changed=true; }
  if(changed) await save();
}

function openOAuthGuide() {
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "GOOGLE AUTH";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent = "읽기 전용 인증 준비";
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="oauth-steps"><div><b>1</b><span>이전에 받은 type: service_account 형식의 JSON 파일을 선택합니다.</span></div><div><b>2</b><span>대상 구글시트는 JSON 안의 client_email 주소에 뷰어로 공유합니다.</span></div><div><b>3</b><span>읽기 전용 미리보기로 학생 수와 결합 오류를 확인합니다.</span></div><div><b>4</b><span>인증 JSON은 Windows 보안 저장소에 암호화 저장되며, 같은 PC에서는 프로그램을 종료 후 다시 실행해도 재선택하지 않습니다.</span></div></div><div class="readonly-rule"><b>요청 권한</b><span>Google Sheets 읽기 전용</span></div><p class="drawer-note">시트를 웹에 공개하거나 링크 공개로 변경할 필요가 없습니다.</p>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
}

async function selectGoogleCredential() {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.selectGoogleCredential)
    return openOAuthGuide();
  const result = await window.schoolBoard.selectGoogleCredential();
  if (result?.canceled) return;
  if (!result?.ok) { googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "Google 서비스 계정 인증을 연결하지 못했습니다."; updateTopSyncStatus(); return toast(googleConnectionError); }
  googleConnectionError = "";
  googleConnectionStatus = await window.schoolBoard.googleCredentialStatus();
  const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
  if (cached?.ok) { readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data); googleConnectionError = ""; }
  else googleConnectionError = cached?.reason || "UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.";
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  startReadonlyAutoRefresh();
  toast(`${result.account || "Google 서비스 계정"}으로 UEP 구글시트에 연결했습니다.`);
  render("settings");
}

async function previewReadonlySync() {
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync)return toast("학교 공용 데이터 연결 기능을 찾지 못했습니다.");
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok){
    const status=await ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify:true}).catch(()=>null);
    googleConnectionStatus={ok:Boolean(status?.authenticated),encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api',endpointReady:status?.endpointReady!==false};
  }
  if(!googleConnectionStatus?.ok)return toast("학교 공용 읽기 연결에 실패했습니다. 로그아웃 후 이름·이메일로 다시 로그인해 주세요.");
  const button = $("[data-readonly-sync]");
  if (button) {
    button.disabled = true;
    button.textContent = "읽는 중…";
  }
  let result=null;
  try{result=await window.schoolBoard.previewReadonlySync();uepDiagRecord('sync.preview.result',result);}
  catch(error){
    uepDiagRecord('sync.preview.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});
    result={ok:false,reason:error?.message||'학교 공용 자료 조회 예외'};
  }
  if (!result?.ok) {
    if (button) {
      button.disabled = false;
      button.textContent = "지금 새로고침";
    }
    googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "동기화하지 못했습니다.";
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    return toast(googleConnectionError);
  }
  googleConnectionError = "";
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  const p = result.preview;
  const n = result.nightPreview || {};
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "GOOGLE SHEETS";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent = "UEP 구글시트 조회 결과";
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="sync-preview"><div><b>${p.masterCount}</b><span>현재원</span></div><div><b>${p.nfcCount ?? "-"}</b><span>NFC 등록</span></div><div><b>${p.dormRosterCount ?? "-"}</b><span>현재 학사생</span></div><div class="${p.unmatchedCount ? "-"}</b><span>학사외출</span></div><div><b>${n.attended ?? "-"}</b><span>야자 출석행</span></div><div class="${n.outsideQr ? "warn" : ""}"><b>${n.outsideQr ?? "-"}</b><span>명단 외 QR</span></div><div><b>${formatSyncTime(result.syncedAt)}</b><span>실시간 조회시각</span></div></div><div class="readonly-rule"><b>기준 구글시트</b><span>${escapeHtml(result.sourceName || readonlyCache?.sourceName || "2026_운호고_UEP_DB_v1.0_Dev")}</span></div><p class="drawer-note">02_학생마스터 학생ID를 기준으로 구글시트 각 탭을 실시간 결합했으며 원본은 변경하지 않았습니다.</p>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");

async function retryGoogleConnection() {
  if (!window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync) return navigate("settings");
  toast("UEP 구글시트를 다시 읽는 중입니다.");
  const result = await window.schoolBoard.previewReadonlySync();
  if (!result?.ok) {
    googleConnectionError = result?.reason || "UEP 구글시트 연결에 실패했습니다.";
    updateTopSyncStatus();
    toast(googleConnectionError);
    render("dashboard");
    return;
  }
  const cached = await window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache();
  if (cached?.ok) {
    readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data);
    googleConnectionError = "";
    toast("UEP 구글시트를 다시 불러왔습니다.");
  } else {
    googleConnectionError = cached?.reason || "UEP 학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.";
    toast(googleConnectionError);
  }
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  render("dashboard");
}

function updateConnectionControls() {
  updateTopSyncStatus();
  const connected = Boolean(readonlyCache?.students?.length && !googleConnectionError);
  const status = $(".connection-title .state");
  if (status && connected) {
    status.textContent = "구글시트 연결됨";
    status.classList.remove("warn", "error");
  }
  const sync = $("[data-readonly-sync]");
  if (sync) sync.disabled = !googleConnectionStatus?.ok;
}

function updateDashboardTimetableControls() {
  const card = $(".timetable-card");
  if (!card) return;
  card.querySelectorAll(".timetable-popup-actions").forEach(node=>node.remove());
}

function shiftTimetableDate(value, amountDays) {
  const base = new Date(`${String(value || dateKey(new Date()))}T12:00:00`);
  if (Number.isNaN(base.getTime())) return dateKey(new Date());
  base.setDate(base.getDate() + Number(amountDays || 0));
  return dateKey(base);
}
